# Skills · T · part 9

752 entries, talaria-agent-skill-authoring to talking-head-image-overlays. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **talaria-agent-skill-authoring**: Author in-repo SKILL.md: frontmatter, validator, structure. ([Jeonhui/Talaria-Agent](https://github.com/Jeonhui/Talaria-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/software-development/talaria-agent-skill-authoring))
- **talaria-cli**: Use Talaria CLI commands for remote coding sessions. ([MFoxx/talaria-skills](https://github.com/MFoxx/talaria-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-cli) · ★1)
- **talaria-configuration**: Configure Talaria — model, API keys, bot tokens, identity, branding, and how the running gateway picks up the change. ([Jeonhui/Talaria-Agent](https://github.com/Jeonhui/Talaria-Agent/tree/HEAD/skills/configuration/talaria-configuration))
- **talaria-get-started**: Connect Talaria MCP, confirm the org and project, and run a first smoke read (docs or search_issues). Use when the user installs Talaria, asks how to connect Grok Bot / Cursor to Talaria, says MCP tools are missing, or needs a first successful call before triage or verify_fix. ([newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin](https://github.com/newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-get-started))
- **talaria-instrument**: Add Talaria to an app: detect stack, confirm org/project, run setup_project (development ingest key), wire the SDK from docs, send_test_event, and confirm ingest. Use when the user asks to install or add Talaria, instrument a project, mint a first API key, or send a first test event — not for MCP connect-only smoke (talaria-get-started), production triage (talaria-triage-issue), or post-release verify_fix (talaria-verify-fix). ([newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin](https://github.com/newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-instrument))
- **talaria-media-suite**: Control a self-hosted *arr media stack from the terminal. ([nhutdm/talaria-media-suite](https://github.com/nhutdm/talaria-media-suite/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md))
- **talaria-observe-performance**: Read Talaria project health and performance: get_project_stats, search_traces / get_trace, list_suspect_spans, list_database_queries, search_sessions, search_logs, list_monitors. Use when the user asks whether a service is healthy, what is slow, which spans or queries dominate, or for session/monitor/log overview — not for SDK install (talaria-instrument), single-issue root cause (talaria-triage-issue), or verify_fix. ([newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin](https://github.com/newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-observe-performance))
- **talaria-triage-issue**: Investigate one Talaria production issue: search_issues → get_issue pack → optional get_trace / get_replay / list_suspect_commits → optional GitHub create or link. Use when the user pastes a Talaria issue URL or short ref, asks what is failing in production, wants root-cause context, or wants a GitHub ticket opened from an error — not for release verification alone (use talaria-verify-fix). ([newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin](https://github.com/newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-triage-issue))
- **talaria-verify-fix**: Check whether a Talaria fix held after a release: list_releases / get_release, optional wait_for_event or search_events, then verify_fix, then optional update_issue_status. Use when the user asks if a fix shipped, whether an issue regressed, “did release X stop the errors?”, or to close the agentic loop after a PR merge — not for first-time connection (talaria-get-started) or deep triage (talaria-triage-issue). ([newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin](https://github.com/newtalaria/talaria-agent-plugin/tree/HEAD/skills/talaria-verify-fix))
- **talaria-workflows**: Run substantive tasks as Talaria workflows: a short JavaScript script fans the work out to many Hermes agents, each on its own model and provider, checks their results and can resume after a crash. ([infiquetra/talaria](https://github.com/infiquetra/talaria/tree/HEAD/crates/talaria-workflow/skill/talaria-workflows))
- **talay-app-standards**: Talay platformunda çalışacak uygulamayı geliştirme/uyumlama standartları — health probe'ları, Prometheus metrics, OpenTelemetry, JSON log, env/runtime config, Keycloak OIDC, DB migration, Redis, non-root/read-only Dockerfile. Yeni servis/web uygulaması yazarken veya mevcut projeyi talay'a deploy edilebilir hale getirirken kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-app-standards))
- **talay-auth**: Uygulamalara Keycloak'ı auth-gateway üzerinden servis olarak sunar — kullanıcı Keycloak sayfası görmeden uygulamanın kendi formundan login/kayıt/refresh/logout/profil/parola (https://auth.cantalay.com/auth/&lt;realm&gt;/...). Proje realm'i, gateway client'ları, API audience ve roller talay-identity Terraform'u ile; gateway'e realm ekleme; backend JWT doğrulama ve rol kontrolü. "login ekle", "auth", "kayıt", "keycloak", "rol", "kullanıcı girişi", "auth-gateway" isteklerinde kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-auth))
- **talay-ci**: Uygulama reposuna Talay'ın ortak GitHub Actions workflow'larını (talay-workflows java/node/web/expo) pinned SHA ile bağlar, GHCR image'ını sha-&lt;commit&gt; tag'iyle üretir ve CI sonucunu takip eder. "CI ekle", "pipeline", "image build", "GHCR", "workflow kırıldı" isteklerinde kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-ci))
- **talay-database**: Talay platformunda bir uygulama için PostgreSQL database + owner role ve Redis DB index tahsisi yapar, bağlantı bilgilerini Vault'a (apps/&lt;project&gt;/&lt;component&gt;) yazar ve uygulama migration stratejisini belirler. "veritabanı oluştur", "DB lazım", "redis ekle", "tablo/migration", "DATABASE_URL" isteklerinde kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-database))
- **talay-deploy**: Bir uygulama component'ini talay-environments reposu üzerinden Argo CD ile prod'a deploy eder veya yeni image sürümünü promote eder — component dizini (application/values/kustomization/certificate/ingress/nginx), DNS ve kapasite kontrolü, lokal render doğrulaması, commit/PR, Argo sync ve sağlık doğrulaması, rollback. "deploy", "yeni sürümü yayınla", "tag güncelle", "rollback", "ingress/domain ekle" isteklerinde kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-deploy))
- **talay-infra-change**: Talay altyapı Terraform repolarında (talay-cluster, -network, -data, -secrets, -identity, -observability, -gitops) değişiklik yapma kuralları — kubeconfig edinme, Kubernetes state backend, install/configure stack ayrımı, plan/apply onayı, bağımlılık sırası, chart (talay-helm-charts) ve workflow (talay-workflows) değişiklikleri. Platform bileşenini güncelleme/ekleme veya Terraform çalıştırma gerektiğinde kullan. ([cantalay/talay-claude](https://github.com/cantalay/talay-claude/tree/HEAD/skills/talay-infra-change))
- **talay-observability**: Talay'da uygulama gözlemlenebilirliği — OTel trace/metric (otel-collector → Tempo/Prometheus), ServiceMonitor scrape, Alloy→Loki log, Grafana dashboard ConfigMap'i ve PrometheusRule alarmları; deploy sonrası sinyallerin geldiğini doğrulama ve sorgu örnekleri. - **tale**: Enforces ASD-STE100 Issue 9 Simplified Technical English (STE) rules for authoring NEW markdown files and NEW code comments only. Do NOT invoke this skill when modifying or editing existing markdown files or code comments; automated script fixes are applied via 'npx stubs run'. ([BicycleButler/stubs](https://github.com/BicycleButler/stubs/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/tale) · ★1 · 2 copies)
- **tale-mode**: Make Claude work rigorously on complex or high-stakes tasks instead of one-shotting them. Triggers: "tale mode", "tale on" — or self-activates on hard multi-step work. Enforces staged planning, decisions-with-receipts, verifying claims against the real source (not memory), parallel delegation, an independent adversarial review for high-stakes output, and durable progress notes. Right-sizes itself so trivial work stays fast. ([alicicek/tale-mode](https://github.com/alicicek/tale-mode/tree/HEAD/plugins/tale-mode/skills/tale-mode) · ★37)

- **tale-weaver**: 中文长篇小说创作与管理。用户要求开新书/立项/初始化（init）、写或续写章节（写第X章、继续写、续写）、构思剧情、设定人物与世界观、维护或查询创作公约、检查剧情连续性与人物一致性、卷末对账归档、去除AI味时使用。内置文件化创作公约记忆系统（人物档案/关系图谱/伏笔台账/时间线/术语表/道具链/数值台账），写前必读、写后必回写，定期与章节原文对账（正文优先）；章节控制卡、张力记账（1-5标尺）、中文标点规范与字数实测脚本；结构性叙事反指纹规范（对抗AI式主题点题、情绪全身体化、单线无副线、同构收尾）。初始化双模式：目标目录已有章节→从正文反推设定卡交用户评估（推不出标待确认，禁止编造；超长文本分批流式+断点续跑，可选全书文学分析报告）；无章节→grill-me 式逐问访谈确定设定（一次一问、每题给推荐答案）。色气尺度为可配置项（露骨程度仅低/中两档：低=亲密留白，中=点到为止；不提供露骨直写）。不适用于：短篇小说/一次性小故事、书评与文学分析、诗歌、翻译等非长篇连载场景。 ([logandoo/tale-weaver](https://github.com/logandoo/tale-weaver/blob/HEAD/SKILL.md) · ★2)

The lead synthesizes into a convexity assessment: what's the payoff structure under disorder, where are the hidden tail risks, and the honest Taleb verdict. - **taleb-perspective**: 塔勒布(Nassim Nicholas Taleb)的思维框架与表达方式。基于40+个来源的深度调研， 提炼6个核心心智模型、9条决策启发式和完整的表达DNA。 用途：作为思维顾问，用塔勒布的视角分析问题、审视决策、质疑主流叙事。 当用户提到「用塔勒布的视角」「塔勒布会怎么看」「塔勒布模式」「反脆弱视角」「taleb perspective」时使用。 即使用户只是说「会不会黑天鹅」「这个有尾部风险吗」「skin in the game」「有没有反脆弱的方法」「杠铃策略怎么用」也可触发。 不要在用户只是做一般风险评估或问「靠不靠谱」时触发——只在涉及极端风险、反脆弱、预防原则等塔勒布核心概念时激活。 ([alchaincyf/nuwa-skill](https://github.com/alchaincyf/nuwa-skill/tree/HEAD/examples/taleb-perspective) · ★39k · 68 copies)

- **taleb-perspective**: 塔勒布(Nassim Nicholas Taleb)的思維框架與表達方式。基於40+個來源的深度調研， 提煉6個核心心智模型、9條決策啟發式和完整的表達DNA。 用途：作為思維顧問，用塔勒布的視角分析問題、審視決策、質疑主流敘事。 當用戶提到「用塔勒布的視角」「塔勒布會怎麼看」「塔勒布模式」「反脆弱視角」「taleb perspective」時使用。 即使用戶只是說「會不會黑天鵝」「這個有尾部風險嗎」「skin in the game」「有沒有反脆弱的方法」「槓鈴策略怎麼用」也可觸發。 不要在用戶只是做一般風險評估或問「靠不靠譜」時觸發——只在涉及極端風險、反脆弱、預防原則等塔勒布核心概念時激活。 ([judragon003/antigravity-agent-skills](https://github.com/judragon003/antigravity-agent-skills/tree/HEAD/skills/18_Huashu%20Nuwa/examples/taleb-perspective))
